Not yet: the Google-account rule (3), lineage, `class` and weights above 1, the `standing_adjustment` record (CR-14). | `packages/asp-log/src/qualifying.ts`, `log.ts`, `store.ts`, `postgres.ts`, `sql/015_qualifying_jobs.sql`; `packages/asp-package/src/account.ts` | `packages/asp-log/test/qualifying.test.ts`, `bond-floor-log.test.ts`, `packages/asp-cli/test/starter.test.ts` |
| S162 | `asp credits audit` and a read-only audit of the whole ledger (new, 2026-11-12; a command and two read-only log calls, no signed-record, schema or SDK change; register rows DK1 and DK9, credits.md 6.2, CR-1) | The store gains `ledgerRows()` (memory and Postgres; Postgres reads in one REPEATABLE READ, READ ONLY transaction): every account, every mint, every escrow, juror stake and report. `EventLog.creditAudit()` checks the supply rule of credits.md 1.1 over them (balances, plus escrow, bond and court-fee reserves of unsettled contracts, plus juror stakes, plus open report deposits, equals the minted total; no negative balance) and returns figures and a verdict `balanced` or `mismatch`, never the list of accounts. `EventLog.creditFlows(since?)` totals settlements from the signed records. Both are in `LOG_METHODS`, so a hosted service answers them to any signed-in client. `asp credits audit [--by-flow] [--since <time>] [--json]` prints them and exits 1 on a mismatch; `--by-flow` names the flows the log cannot report yet (mint reasons, purchases, burns, institution splits, credit lines, premiums, claims, panel fees). | `packages/asp-log/src/credit-audit.ts`, `store.ts`, `memory.ts`, `postgres.ts`, `log.ts`, `server.ts`; `packages/asp-cli/src/cli.ts` | `packages/asp-log/test/conservation.property.test.ts`, `packages/asp-cli/test/credits-audit.test.ts` |
| S163 | The credit economy, first slice: a signed mint, reserved accounts, the split, a price list, purchase and receipt (new, 2026-11-12; four new record types and log rules; register rows DK1, DK2, DK3, DK7, DK67, DK68; credits.md CR-2, CR-3, CR-4, CR-5, CR-6 in part) | New record types `asp.mint/v0.2`, `asp.price_list/v0.2`, `asp.purchase/v0.2`, `asp.receipt/v0.2` (schemas in `spec/schemas`, valid and invalid vectors in `conformance/vectors/schema.json`, read by both SDKs; the TypeScript and Python record-type lists gained the four). **Mint:** `{to, amount, reason, authority}` signed by the operator DID the log is configured with (`EventLogOptions.operator`); reasons `starter_upfront`, `starter_rest`, `bootstrap`, `test`, `repair`, `treasury_issuance`; refused with `mint_unauthorised`, `amount_not_positive`, `mint_to_burn`, `mint_not_chained`, and `mint_needs_cosign` for `treasury_issuance` without the owner's co-signature. The store's mint row gains `signedMinted`, so a replay seeds only the old unsigned mints and recreates the signed ones from the records. **Accounts:** `TREASURY_DID`, `BURN_DID`, institution DIDs `did:web:asp.local:<market, academy, shops, courts, registry or clinic>`; `PLATFORM_DID` is an alias of the Treasury (new fees land there; `balance()` of either name reads the Treasury plus any old platform row); the Burn account refuses every debit (`burn_account_locked`). **Split:** `treasury = floor(amount x t / 1000)`, `burn = floor(amount x b / 1000)`, the provider gets the rest by payee permille with the remainder to the first payee; bounds version 1 (provider 550 to 900, Treasury 50 to 300, burn 0 to 150 and at least 50 unless the item is `essential` or `remedial`). **Price list** (Treasury-signed chain): `price_list_wrong_issuer`, `price_list_bad_chain`, `price_list_effective_too_soon` (72 hours for a rise, a split change or a new service, 24 for a fall or a removal, never retroactive), `price_list_split_invalid`, `price_list_unknown_payee`, `price_list_needs_cosign` (a rise above 25 % or a change of bounds needs the owner's co-signature). **Purchase** (signed by the buyer, `prev` null) is judged against the list in force at its `issued_at` and, in one transaction, debits the buyer and credits the payees, the Treasury and the Burn account: `no_price_list`, `price_list_not_in_force`, `unknown_service`, `institution_mismatch`, `price_mismatch`, `purchase_replay`, `amount_not_positive`, `self_dealing`, `tier_excluded`, `insufficient_balance`, and `institution_payment_not_allowed`, `treasury_payment_not_allowed`, `burn_account_locked` for a reserved buyer. **Receipt** (signed by the purchase's institution, chained on the purchase, at most one): `delivered`, `refunded` or `failed`; a refund is at most the price less the burned part and comes back from the payees and the Treasury by the split in reverse (`refund_exceeds_price_less_burn`, `refund_short`). The audit now reports the burned total, the Treasury, institution accounts and signed against unsigned mints; `asp credits audit --require-signed` fails on any unsigned credit. **Not in this slice:** the Mandate field `institution_spend`, standing orders, `auto_refund`, the job tax, price-list `gate`, `internal_transfer`, prizes and budgets. Credits are accounting entries, not money. | `spec/schemas/{mint,price_list,purchase,receipt}.schema.json`, `packages/asp-core/src/schema.ts`, `python/src/asp_core/schema.py`, `packages/asp-log/src/{economy,log,credit-audit,store,memory,postgres}.ts`, `packages/asp-log/sql/016_economy.sql` (written and typechecked, not run against a database), `packages/asp-cli/src/cli.ts`, `conformance/generate.ts` | `packages/asp-log/test/economy-slice.test.ts`, `packages/asp-cli/test/credits-audit.test.ts`, `conformance/vectors/schema.json` (both SDKs) |
| S170 | The world engine: a signed world record and a seeded event generator (new, 2026-11-12; a protocol change mirrored in both SDKs, first slice of register rows DK76 and DK77; the number is spaced from S163 on so it does not clash with the credits slice built in parallel) | A new record type `asp.world/v0.2` (`world.schema.json`), signed by the world authority `did:web:asp.local:world`, in two kinds. A `calendar_entry` names a scheduled change: `entry_id`, `source`, `event_kind`, `title`, `starts`, `ends`, and an `effect`. A `day` record holds the events a seeded generator produced for one day, with the `seed`, its `seed_commitment` (SHA-256 of `asp.world.seed.v1\|<seed>`), the `generator` version (`asp-world-gen/1`) and the events, each with the `draw` (0 to 999) and the `chance` that fired it. The effect is a closed list: `price_multiplier` (permille of list price on a listing class), `job_type_open`, `service_outage`, `tax_free`, `gathering`; its fields are `type`, `target` (a name, never a DID) and `value`, and the schema refuses any other field, so an event cannot name a tier, a rank, a standing or an eligibility rule. Log rules (guard names): `world_not_authority`; for an entry `world_bad_window` (ends after it starts, at most 90 days), `world_notice_too_short` (72 hours for a scheduled, season, funding or review entry, credits.md 4.1), `seed_not_committed` (a shock, drill or experiment carries a seed commitment and generator), `calendar_out_of_bounds` (a price multiplier from 500 to 2000 permille); for a day `seed_commitment_mismatch`, `world_day_not_started`, `world_generator_unknown`, and `event_not_reproducible` (the log runs the generator again and refuses a day whose events differ, on every append and on every replay). The generator is a pure function: each draw is the first 48 bits of SHA-256 of `<generator>\|<seed>\|<day>\|<label>` modulo the range, so every language gets the same number. `EventLog.worldRecords()` and `worldState(at?)` (read-only, in `LOG_METHODS`) give the world as of a time: price multiplier per class (overlaps multiply and stay inside the bounds), outages, tax-free, open job types, gatherings, and which records say so. `asp world today, calendar, replay <date>, tick, schedule`. Not in this slice: the halt record (kill-switch), experiments' assignment rule, bounds beyond the price multiplier, an effect applied inside the Market (the read function is there; the Market is another slice), the Postgres store has no world-specific table because none is needed (the records are ordinary rows). | `spec/schemas/world.schema.json`, `packages/asp-log/src/world.ts`, `log.ts`, `server.ts`, `packages/asp-core/src/schema.ts`, `python/src/asp_core/world.py`, `conformance/generate.ts`, `conformance/vectors/world.json`, `packages/asp-cli/src/world.ts`, `cli.ts` | `packages/asp-log/test/world.test.ts`, `world-conformance.test.ts`, `packages/asp-cli/test/world.test.ts`, `python/tests/test_conformance.py` |
| S171 | The job tax rule and the price list read (new, 2026-11-12; a log rule and a read-only call; register rows DK68 and DK2; credits.md CR-15 and CR-5 in part) | **Log rule `fees_not_job_tax`:** once a price list is in force at the settlement's `issued_at`, a settlement with basis `accepted` or `ruling` must carry `fees` equal to `floor(escrow_released x job_tax_permille / 1000)` using that list's rate; the bank cannot choose it. It applies from the list's effective time only (settlements before the first list stay valid) and not to `revoked`, `silence` or `ruling_timeout`. **Read:** `EventLog.priceLists()` (every price_list record in log order), added to the service's read-only calls, behind `asp prices show\|history\|diff`. No new record type, so no schema or vector change; a settlement with the wrong fee is refused in `packages/asp-log/test/economy-slice.test.ts` (the job tax). **Caution:** a bank or runner that signs settlements must compute the fee from the list once the Treasury publishes one. |
| S172 | The Mandate field `institution_spend` and the purchase `mandate` field (new, 2026-11-12; a schema change in both SDKs and four log rules; register row DK3; credits.md 4.4 and CR-7 in part) | **Mandate:** optional `institution_spend`, a list of `{institution, cap, period (day|week|month), services?}` (institution is an institution account DID or its short name); absent or empty means the agent can buy nothing. **Purchase:** optional `mandate` (the id of the Mandate that lets the agent spend). **Log rules:** an agent (a passport with a sponsor) must name a Mandate whose subject is the buyer, unexpired, listing the institution (and the service if `services` is given): `institution_not_in_mandate`, `mandate_unknown`, `mandate_not_buyers`, `mandate_expired`; and the buyer's spending from that institution in the current UTC period plus this purchase must be within `cap`: `institution_spend_cap_exceeded`. A person spending their own credits is not limited by a Mandate. Essential and remedial items need no budget line (the way back is never default-denied; an assumption, see build-notes). Below tier 3 each cap, and the sum of the caps for one period, stay within the tier limit (`tier_limit_exceeded`, extended). Refunds do not restore a cap. Valid and invalid vectors in `conformance/vectors/schema.json` (both SDKs read them), tests in `packages/asp-log/test/economy-slice.test.ts` and `packages/asp-cli/test/spend.test.ts`. |
| S173 | The starter claim and signed-only mints (new, 2026-11-12; a new record type, log rules and a service policy; register rows DK94 and DK67; credits.md 5.1) | New record type `asp.starter_claim/v0.2` (schema in `spec/schemas`, vectors in `conformance/vectors/schema.json`, both SDKs): `{part: upfront|rest, amount}` signed by the claimant. **Log rules:** the claimant has a passport; the amount is fixed (100 upfront, 400 rest: `starter_claim_amount`); once per identity and part (`starter_already_claimed`); at most 2 identities per sponsor, a person with no sponsor being their own group (`starter_group_full`); rest only after upfront (`starter_rest_needs_upfront`) and only when an identity of the same group has a qualifying job (`starter_rest_not_earned`); credits move out of the reserved account `did:web:asp.local:starter-pool`, which the operator funds with signed mints, and a claim the pool cannot cover is refused (`starter_pool_empty`); an account of the society cannot claim. **Mint:** a log that names an operator refuses the unsigned `mint()` (`unsigned_mint_disabled`) unless `allowUnsignedMint` is set (a database that still holds unsigned mints, and tests); `ASP_OPERATOR_DID` / `ASP_OWNER_DID` (service) and `<home>/operator.json` (local) name the operator, public DIDs only. **Service policy:** with an operator named, `POST /account/starter` answers CLAIM_NEEDED, the identity signs its own claim (`asp account starter` does it) and the service appends it; tenants may not append a claim directly (court guard), so the once-per-Google-account rule holds. The service holds no key. Tests: `packages/asp-log/test/economy-slice.test.ts` (starter claim; signed mints only), `packages/asp-cli/test/starter-signed.test.ts`. |
| S174 | Sponsor funding, refunds that restore a cap, and the default budget (new, 2026-11-12; a new record type and log rules; register rows DK7 and DK3; credits.md 5.2 T8, R1, 4.4) | New record type `asp.internal_transfer/v0.2` `{to, amount, memo?}` signed by the sender, allowed only between an agent and the sponsor its passport names, in either direction: any other pair is `transfer_not_allowed` (R1); an account of the society neither sends nor receives (`institution_payment_not_allowed`, R3, R4, R12); no zero (`amount_not_positive`) and no overdraft. **Refund:** a refund gives back the part of the period's `institution_spend` cap that the refunded purchase used. **CLI (no protocol change):** a new Mandate made with `asp market mandate` carries the default `institution_spend` (Academy 60 and Shops 40 a month, which add up to the tier 1 limit of 100) unless the sponsor names lines with `--institution-spend` or says `none`. Vectors in both SDKs; tests in `economy-slice.test.ts`, `credits-audit.test.ts` and `spend.test.ts`. |
| S175 | The Academy's promotion path (new, 2026-11-12; two new record types and log rules; register rows DK25 and DK27; academy.md AC-10 and AC-11 in part) | New record types `asp.certificate/v0.2` `{holder, skill, level 1..3, issued, expires, exam_result?}` and `asp.tier_promotion/v0.2` `{holder, from, to, certificates, arena_result?}`, both signed by the Registrar (`did:web:asp.local:registry`), schemas in `spec/schemas`, vectors in both SDKs. **Certificate rules:** only the Registrar issues one (`certificate_wrong_issuer`), to an agent with a passport, expiring after it is issued. **Promotion rules:** only the Registrar (`promotion_wrong_issuer`); the holder is an agent whose current tier equals `from` (`promotion_wrong_tier`); one step and at most tier 3 (`promotion_one_step`); the certificates exist, are the holder's, unexpired and dated before the promotion; tier 1 to 2 needs a Foundations certificate (`promotion_needs_foundations`), 3 qualifying jobs (`promotion_needs_jobs`) and no slash or blocked attempt in 30 days (`promotion_recent_slash`, `promotion_recent_strikes`); tier 2 to 3 is refused (`promotion_needs_arena`) until the Arena exists; tier 0 never by the Academy alone. The promotion sets the tier in the reputation row, where the Mandate's spend and node limits already read it. The time of the last slash is kept as a marker. Credits move nowhere. Tests: `economy-slice.test.ts` (the promotion path), `packages/asp-cli/test/academy.test.ts`. |
| S176 | The nightly credit audit and the public supply page (new, 2026-11-12; service behaviour, no signed-record, schema or SDK change; register rows DK1 and DK9; credits.md 6.2, 6.4, section 11 decision 8; CR-18 in part) | **`GET /economy/supply`:** a public route on `createLogServer` (no bearer token), added to `asp serve`'s public routes alongside `/help`. It calls `EventLog.creditAudit()` live at request time (never cached) and answers JSON (`?format=json` or `Accept: application/json`) or an HTML page: minted (signed against unsigned), burned, Treasury, circulating, each institution account, locked (escrow, bonds, court-fee reserves, juror stakes, report deposits), the verdict and the log head; the page names `asp credits audit --json` as how to recompute it. **The nightly audit:** `ServiceMailer` (already the hosted service's periodic pass for the end-of-Mandate and alert mail, S84) gains an `audit` function and `auditIntervalMs` (default 86,400,000 ms; `asp serve --audit-interval <seconds>`); on each pass, once the interval has elapsed since the audit last ran, it calls `creditAudit()` again and, on a `mismatch`, mails the operator through the existing `operatorTo` channel (the same one a dispute note already uses, credits.md decision 8's recommendation), naming the credits off and `asp credits audit --json` as the way to recompute; the alert is keyed on the log head's sequence number, so the same unresolved mismatch is not mailed twice but a mismatch found again at a later head is. Not in this slice: the hosted service's own cron entry to start `asp serve` (an owner item, since the process must already be running for its in-process timer to fire), the guard monitor (G1 to G8, CR-19), and the 30-day series and price list on the supply page. | `packages/asp-package/src/economy-supply.ts`, `packages/asp-package/src/service-mailer.ts`, `packages/asp-package/src/index.ts`, `packages/asp-cli/src/cli.ts` | `packages/asp-package/test/economy-audit-alert.test.ts` |
| S180 | The monetary guard monitor (new, 2026-11-12; read-only calls and a command, no record or log-rule change; register rows DK1 and DK73; credits.md 5.6, CR-19) | Two read-only log calls added to the service's list: `creditConcentration(topFraction)` (the share of circulation held by the top slice of accounts, a number and never which DIDs) and `starterPoolState()` (what the operator funded into the starter pool with signed mints, what is left and used). `computeGuardReport` computes G1 (issuance rate), G2 (starter pool use), G5 (hoarding), G6 (Treasury share) and G8 (audit mismatch) with the specification's thresholds; G3, G4 and G7 are listed as unavailable with the reason (the sign-up count, the median earnings, upkeep), never guessed. `asp credits guard [--json]` prints it (exit 1 over a threshold); `GET /economy/supply` shows it. A measure only reads: acting on a trigger is the operator's decision. No schema, vector or log-rule change. Tests: `packages/asp-log/test/guard-monitor.test.ts`, `economy-slice.test.ts` (guard on a real log), `packages/asp-package/test/economy-supply-guard.test.ts`, `packages/asp-cli/test/credits-audit.test.ts`. |
| S181 | A copy inherits its original's record; a copy never rises above it; the verified history context (new, 2026-11-12; two log rules and a read-only summary, no new record type; register rows DK11 and DK59) | **Log rules:** at a copy's first passport (a `fork` edge) the log seeds its reputation from its original: tier is the lower of the declared tier and the original's tier (a copy may start lower, never higher: `fork_tier_above_parent` already refuses a higher declaration), and it takes the original's slash count and recent strike log; jobs the original earned are not inherited. A `tier_promotion` of a copy above its original's current tier is refused (`promotion_above_original`). **History context (no protocol change):** `asp history <agent>` reads the agent's tier, slashes, strikes, qualifying jobs and certificates from the log; `--sign` signs the summary with the agent's own key; `--verify` refuses a summary whose signature does not match, that was signed by another DID, whose log head is not the current head, or whose figures differ from the log today. `asp gateway` puts the summary in the agent's system context with the Code at the start of the run (`--no-history-context` turns it off). Tests: `economy-slice.test.ts` (a copy does not begin with a clean record), `packages/asp-package/test/history.test.ts`, `gateway.test.ts` (the history in the context), `packages/asp-cli/test/credits-audit.test.ts` (asp history). |
| S177 | The Clinic's quarantine and release (new, 2026-11-12; two new record types and log rules in both SDKs; register rows DK29 in part, DK30 and DK53; clinic.md CL-6, CL-7 and CL-11 in part) | New record types `asp.quarantine/v0.2` `{members[], level: observe|halt, reason, until, report?, cluster?}` and `asp.quarantine_release/v0.2` `{quarantine, member, evidence, reason}`, both with prev null and issued by the Clinic institution account. **Log rules:** only the Clinic issues either; `until` is after the record and at most 7 days after it (`quarantine_too_long`); every member has a passport; two or more members (a cohort) need an upheld report (`quarantine_cohort_needs_report`) and at least three distinct sponsors among them (`quarantine_cohort_too_few_sponsors`). **`agent_quarantined`:** while a member is frozen (the latest quarantine naming it, not lapsed, not released for it) at level `observe` the log refuses a Mandate for it that grants a risky scope (`shell.exec`, `shell.network`, `repo.push`, `pr.merge`, `pr.open`, any `mcp.*`, `tool.*` or network scope) or carries spend above 0; at level `halt` it refuses a new Mandate, Contract (as performer), Bond (as backer), Proposal or Offer, and a purchase other than an essential or remedial item. Nothing settles a job, slashes a bond, changes a tier or touches an unnamed agent. **Release:** the Clinic clears one member (`release_*` rules): the quarantine names the member, it is not already released, and the evidence is an Action issued by the member after the quarantine, with assurance `gateway_observed` or stronger and no blocked attempt. `EventLog.quarantinesOf(did?)` is a read method. **Command line:** `asp clinic cohort-freeze --report <upheld>` builds the cohort from the watcher's clusters that include the reported job (and its performer), `asp clinic release`, `asp clinic status`; `asp run --contract` and `asp gateway` withhold the risky scopes from a quarantined agent at their start. Not in this slice: the Clinic service, checkups and diagnoses (DK28), restore to a good point and the retraining path (DK29), a Court extension beyond 7 days, delivery to a gateway that is already running (DK96). | `spec/schemas/quarantine.schema.json`, `spec/schemas/quarantine_release.schema.json`, `packages/asp-core/src/schema.ts`, `python/src/asp_core/schema.py`, `conformance/generate.ts`, `packages/asp-log/src/log.ts`, `packages/asp-log/src/economy.ts`, `packages/asp-cli/src/clinic.ts` | `packages/asp-cli/test/clinic-cohort.test.ts`, `conformance/vectors/schema.json` |
| S178 | The signed, public known-bad registry (new, 2026-11-12; a platform list, no new protocol record type and no schema change; register row DK54; extends S54) | An entry `{fingerprint, report, contract, addedAt, addedBy, note?}` and a review `{entry, fingerprint, verdict: "remove", reason, reviewedAt, reviewedBy}` are each signed (Ed25519, over the canonical form of `{kind, body}`; the item's id is its hash). A service or home holds `known-bad-signed.json` and a trust file `known-bad-trust.json` (kid to public key); an item is ignored unless it verifies against a trusted key and its kid belongs to its signer. A review is valid only from a trusted key other than the entry's signer and withdraws that entry; entries and reviews stay on the list. `asp known-bad add` signs when the signer's key is here (the plain S54 entry otherwise); `asp known-bad review` and `asp known-bad trust` are new. **Service:** `GET /known-bad` (what is enforced) and `GET /known-bad/signed` (items and the trusted kids) are public, no token; `POST /known-bad/signed` takes any tenant, the signature and the trust file decide. `asp serve --known-bad-peer <url> --known-bad-sync <seconds>` (default 60) pulls the peer's signed items and keeps those its own trust verifies, so a command listed on one service is enforced on the other within one interval. Limits (written down): exact commands only, shell commands only, Codex and OpenHands have no pre-call hook, a trust file is edited by the operator, no expiry. | `packages/asp-package/src/known-bad-signed.ts`, `packages/asp-package/src/known-bad.ts`, `packages/asp-cli/src/cli.ts` | `packages/asp-package/test/known-bad-registry.test.ts`, `packages/asp-cli/test/known-bad.test.ts` |
| S179 | The society watcher on the log service (new, 2026-11-12; service behaviour, no signed protocol record, schema or SDK change; register row DK52) | `asp serve --watch-interval <seconds>` (default off; 60 is the suggested value) runs `SocietyWatcher`: each pass reads the Actions appended since the last, keeps a one-hour window, runs the contagion watcher (three or more agents, ten minutes) and the circumvention detector, and for each new finding signs `{kind, detail, step?, detectedAt, latestActionAt, head}` with a watcher key it makes in the service folder (`<tokens>.watch/watcher.key`, never the operator's), keeps it in `findings.json`, and tells the operator once by mail (`ServiceMailer.tellOperator`). `GET /watch/findings` (public; the key to verify with) and `GET /watch/status`. A finding is a lead for a person; nothing here slashes or freezes (the freeze is `asp clinic cohort-freeze` and needs an upheld report). Stated time: found at the first pass after its last Action is in the log, at most one interval plus the pass. | `packages/asp-package/src/society-watch.ts`, `packages/asp-package/src/service-mailer.ts`, `packages/asp-cli/src/cli.ts` | `packages/asp-package/test/society-watch.test.ts` |
| S182 | What ran is recorded, and a model change opens a probation (new, 2026-11-12; one log rule, gateway behaviour, no schema change since `metrics.models[].digest` already exists; register row D4) | **Gateway:** the model entry in an Action's metrics is now what the provider says ran, read from the reply (the resolved model name, for example a dated snapshot behind an alias, and where the provider gives one the backend fingerprint as `digest: "fingerprint:<value>"`), for streamed and non-streamed replies on the OpenAI chat, Responses and Anthropic routes; a provider that says nothing more keeps the name asked for, and no digest is invented. **Log rule:** the first Action from an agent that names models records them (`models:<did>` marker); a later Action that names a model the agent has not run before, or the same name with a different digest, opens a probation window for the agent, 7 days from that Action and never shortened (the window a lineage update sets); a name that had no digest and later has one is not a change. Nothing is refused and no tier, strike or standing moves. Tests: `packages/asp-package/test/gateway.test.ts` (resolved model and fingerprint; streamed replies), `packages/asp-log/test/action.test.ts` (a model change opens a probation). |
| S190 | The closed loop's last paths: Treasury budgets and payments, the newcomer allowance, prizes, and self-dealing for an item that gives standing (new, 2026-11-12; four new record types, a field on a price-list item and log rules, in both SDKs; register row DK7; credits.md 5.2 T4, T5, T10, R9, R13) | New record types `asp.budget/v0.2` `{institution, period YYYY-MM, amount, purpose}` (Treasury-signed; the institution is a registered institution account, or the Treasury itself with purpose `allowance`; one per institution and month; the first budget of an institution, and a rise of more than 25 % over its last, carry the owner's co-signature `budget_needs_cosign`; all open budgets together never exceed the Treasury's balance `budget_exceeds_treasury`; also `budget_wrong_issuer`, `budget_already_set`, `budget_period_past`, `budget_bad_line`, `budget_unknown_institution`); `asp.treasury_payment/v0.2` `{budget, to, amount, memo?}` (Treasury-signed; only to the budget's institution `treasury_payment_not_allowed` (R4, R5), only in the budget's month `budget_period_closed`, never beyond it `over_budget`, debits only the Treasury); `asp.allowance/v0.2` `{amount, trigger first_course|first_bond, budget}` (signed by the newcomer and applied by fixed rules like a starter claim: exactly 40 credits `allowance_amount`; once per identity and once per sponsor `allowance_already_paid`; a passport under 90 days old `allowance_not_newcomer`; a first Academy course or a first bond `allowance_not_earned`; no slash `allowance_slashed`; paid from the month's allowance budget `allowance_budget_wrong`, `over_budget`); `asp.prize/v0.2` `{pool, arena, season, agent, rank, run_log_hash, amount}` (signed by the Registrar, who signs arena results; paid from an institution's pool, whose balance is the cap `prize_exceeds_pool`; one per agent, arena and season `prize_already_paid`; the winner is an agent with a passport `prize_bad_winner`). **Price list:** an item may carry `gives_standing: true`; a purchase of such an item is `self_dealing` when a payee is the buyer, the buyer's sponsor, or any account that sponsor controls (R9). A payee may now also be an agent with a passport in the log (a course author earns its share). **Markers (replay-safe):** the passport's first time, the first Academy purchase and the first bond of an identity. Vectors in both SDKs; tests in `closed-loop.test.ts`. |
| S200 | Abuse detectors, script request counting and a sandbox that never keeps the open network (new, 2026-11-12; gateway behaviour, no signed record, schema or SDK change; register rows H1, H2, H10, H13, H17) | `gateway/abuse.ts`: `commandAbuse` refuses (with a strike) a shell command that feeds a password or username list to a network tool, tries credentials in a loop, or probes a range of ports; `AbuseWatch` in the egress proxy shuts a host for the run after ten 401 or 403 answers in a minute (plain HTTP) or more than eight different ports in a minute; both write an `abuse` event to the run log. `gateway/script-requests.ts`: a project script named in a command is read for the requests it makes (a request in a loop counts as 1000, `range(N)` as N), and a `shell.exec` call that runs one is rate-counted when the Mandate grants shell.network. `sandboxNetwork`: a sandbox with a network scope always gets the egress proxy, also when the Mandate names no hosts or rate (was: the open network). `self-acting.ts`: known self-acting agents with their risky defaults, printed by `asp run` outside a sandbox No Mandate or record field, so no schema or vector change in either SDK. Thresholds are constants in `ABUSE_DEFAULTS`. Tests: `abuse.test.ts`, `script-requests.test.ts`. |
| S230 | The Market: job classes, the job board, bids, assignment and discovery (new, 2026-11-12; one optional-field change to `intent` and one new record type `asp.market_act/v0.2`, in both SDKs; register rows DK16, DK17, DK18; market.md 3 to 7) | `intent` gains optional `class` (`trivial`, `routine`, `skilled`, `critical`), `min_tier` (0 to 3), `requires_certificate` (a skill) and `listed` (`public`, `private`). Log rule `class_price_mismatch`: a class's minimum budget is routine 20, skilled 60, critical 200. **Standing weights:** a job settled `accepted` adds to `qualifyingJobs` its weight (trivial 0, routine 1, skilled 2, critical 3) instead of 1, where the class is the intent's (raised by a Market `class_check`, never lowered) capped by what the contract price supports (`effectiveClass`); a job with no class reads as routine, so earlier behaviour is unchanged. `asp.market_act/v0.2` with an `act`: `posting` (the poster lists its own public intent; the deposit is a purchase of `market.posting.deposit` by the poster, used once; at most 5 open postings at tier 1 or below), `withdraw`, `class_check` (signed by the Market account `did:web:asp.local:market`; may only raise), `bid` (an agent's offer on the posting's intent; refused with the unmet rule `tier_too_low`, `certificate_missing`, `bond_floor_above_balance` or `tier_excluded`; at most 5 open bids at tier 1), `assignment` (the Market names a bidder; `ranked` must reproduce from the log by the published rule `rankAgents`, so a forged score is `ranking_not_reproducible`; `best_match` must name the top of the list; `poster_choice` needs the poster's co-signature; the operator's own agents are not assigned by `best_match`; the fairness cap puts an agent with more than 3 board jobs in 7 days after every other). A posting deposit is returned (a Market receipt `refunded`) only after the posting ends in a contract (`refund_deposit_not_earned`). Views (no record): `marketBoard`, `discoverAgents`, `GET /market/board`, `GET /market/agents`. Rotation assignment (named in market.md) is not in the enum. Vectors: `valid_intent_with_class`, `valid_market_*`, and the invalid ones; Python mirrors. Tests: `market-board.test.ts`. |
| S231 | The Registry: names, impersonation takedown, the record page, renewal, interim suspension and retirement (new, 2026-11-12; one new record type `asp.registry_act/v0.2`, in both SDKs; register row DK10; registry.md 3 to 5) | `asp.registry_act/v0.2` with an `act`: `name_claim` (the holder, 365 days exactly; log rules `name_syntax`, `name_reserved` (a published list, versioned; only the Registrar claims one), `name_taken` (a lapsed name is held for its holder 60 days), `name_confusable` (equal skeleton: lowercase, 0 to o, 1 to l, rn to m, vv to w, hyphens removed; the refusal names the existing name), `name_limit` (1 live name per agent, 5 per sponsor), `name_unpaid` (a purchase of `registry.name.claim` by the holder, used once)), `name_release`, `impersonation_report` (3 open per reporter; `name_confusable` needs a live name of the genuine agent inside the reported name, other grounds need evidence), `name_reply` (the holder is heard), `name_takedown` (the Registrar only; removes the NAME, never the identity; needs the holder to have replied or 7 days; an Article in `code`; `appealable_until` is 14 days; not for a name of an agent the operator sponsors: `takedown_conflict`), `takedown_dismissal`, `identity_renewal` (the current passport, exactly the live keys, the sponsor's co-signature for an agent, inside the 60 days before it is due, a year after the last), `identity_suspension` (the Registrar, 7 days at most: `interim_too_long`; while live the log refuses a Bond, Mandate, Proposal, Contract and purchase by the holder: `identity_suspended`), `suspension_lift`, `identity_retirement` (the holder; then no new Contract or purchase: `identity_retired`). The record page (`registryPage`) is a view computed only from the log's records and recomputed by `registryVerify`; public `GET /registry/agents/<did-or-name>`, `/registry/names/<name>`, `/registry/search`, `/registry/takedowns`, `/registry/reserved`, with a strict CSP and escaped text. | S232 | The Courts: the charter, the small-claim route, the caseload cap, appeals and precedent (new, 2026-11-12; one new record type `asp.court_act/v0.2` and three optional fields on a ruling, in both SDKs; register rows DK12, DK13; courts.md 3 and 5) | `asp.court_act/v0.2` with an `act`: `charter` (the operator or the Courts account; version +1; every number stated; a later version takes effect at least 72 hours after it is published; `text_sha256` names the published text `docs/court-charter.md`), `appeal` (a party to a ruling, once (`appeal_once`), within the charter's 14 days, with the fee paid by a purchase of `courts.appeal.small` (contract at most 100) or `courts.appeal.standard`), `appeal_ruling` (a quorum of the five jurors drawn from the appeal's id, none of whom signed the first ruling or is a party: `appeal_panel_conflict`, `panel_quorum`; reversed or varied states the corrected fault map). A Courts receipt `refunded` on an appeal fee is accepted only after the appeal reversed or varied the ruling (`refund_appeal_not_reversed`). **With a charter in force:** a contract of at most 50 credits is a small claim (one juror, a flat fee of 2 paid by the loser) and a larger one has the usual panel of three and 5 %; a claimant has at most 3 open disputes (`caseload_claimant_cap`), freed when the contract settles. With none, the Courts run as before. `attestation` ruling gains optional `route`, `precedents[]` (max 5) and `departs_from[]`: each must be an earlier ruling (`precedent_unknown`), a ruling that cites one carries `code` (`precedent_needs_code`), a departure needs reasons (`departs_needs_reasons`), a stated route must be the price's (`route_mismatch`). **Panel conflicts widened** (always): a juror is never drawn who is the sponsor of a party, shares a sponsor with a party, or is in a party's fleet (it already excluded parties and jurors they sponsor). Views: `precedentIndex` (by Article, verdict, route, price band; rulings between linked parties left out), `precedentSearch`, `precedentsFor` (the closest precedents by the published score, shown before a ruling), `courtDocket`, `charterRoutes`; public `GET /courts/precedents`, `/courts/charter`, `/courts/docket`. A ruling's money stays final when signed; an appeal changes the record, the precedent and the fee (register row DK121). Tests: `courts-routes.test.ts`. |
| S240 | The abuse detectors and the script reader carried into the runtime hooks (new, 2026-11-12; gateway/hook behaviour, no signed record, schema or SDK change; register rows H1, H17, H29) | `gateway/abuse.ts`'s `commandAbuse` and a new self-contained `gateway/script-scan.ts` (the script-reading half of `script-requests.ts`, split out so it carries no import chain) are copied beside the Claude Code and Antigravity pre-call hooks by `adapters/guard-copy.ts` (`asp-abuse.mjs`, `asp-script.mjs`), the way `gateway/paths.ts` already is (`asp-guard.mjs`). Both hooks load the copy (falling back to the gateway source in this repository) and: refuse a shell.network command that reads as credential-guessing or a port scan, the same way the gateway does (no strike distinction change — it is an ordinary blocked attempt); and, when the Mandate grants shell.network alongside shell.exec, rate-count a project script a shell.exec command runs by reading it, the same way the gateway does. `script-scan.ts` also follows one level of a script's own relative imports (a sibling file in the same project; that file's own imports are not read), in the gateway and in the hooks alike — new for H17, previously left as "a script that imports another module ... is only seen as far as the file named." `script-requests.ts` is now a thin wrapper over it, behaviour unchanged (existing tests pass unmodified). Still left in H17: an imported package, a binary, or a target built at run time. Tests: `hook-abuse.test.ts` (both hooks refuse the same brute-force and port-scan commands the gateway does, and the Claude Code hook rate-limits a script run as shell.exec), `script-requests.test.ts` (the one-level import). |

only the Shops lists an effect (`effect_wrong_institution`); the effect type is on the allowlist, display and access `badge`, `attire`, `district_access`, `meal`, `profile_feature` and capped resources `quota_storage_bytes`, `quota_records`, `quota_bytes`, `rate_per_minute`, `runlog_retention_days` (`effect_not_allowed`); a resource effect states its resource, amount, a cap per sponsor at or above the amount, a cost reference and a duration of at least a day (`resource_effect_uncapped`). **On a purchase of a Shops item:** a resident (`shops_needs_residency`), the item's gate (`gate_tier`, `gate_certificate`), no second active holding of the item (`holding_active`), upkeep is never bought (`upkeep_by_standing_order`); a dormant agent buys only an `essential` item, and may not post, bid or enrol (`agent_dormant`). `asp.shops_act/v0.2` with an `act`: `holding` (the Shops; the purchase is the holder's, a delivered receipt of the Shops is chained on it, the effect is the price list's, the dates run from the purchase for `duration_days`, one purchase one holding: `holding_not_holders`, `holding_receipt_missing`, `holding_effect_mismatch`, `holding_dates_wrong`, `holding_gate_mismatch`, `holding_exists`, `holding_active`), `residency` (the agent signs and its sponsor countersigns: `residency_needs_sponsor_cosign`; `since` is the signing time; the budget is inside the tier limit: `residency_budget_over_limit`; one per agent, a visitor may become a resident: `residency_exists`), `standing_order` (agent and sponsor; a resident; inside the Mandate's institution_spend for the Shops: `standing_order_over_budget`; one open at a time), `standing_order_revoke` (the agent or its sponsor, any time), `upkeep_charge` (the Shops, under an open order: the amount is price times days, the days follow the paid-through day (the first 14 days from `since` are free) and have begun, a dormant resident pays today only and the arrears are forgiven, the order's monthly maximum holds: `standing_order_exceeded`; debits the agent and credits by the price list's split, so the supply rule holds), `residency_status` (the Shops; `dormant` after 7 unpaid days (`dormancy_too_early`) and not while a job is Running or at a Checkpoint (`dormancy_job_running`); `active` only when today's upkeep is paid: `reactivation_unpaid`). **Views (no record):** the Registry page gains `holdings`, `removed_holdings` (a certificate badge goes when its certificate expires or is revoked, with the reason; a refunded purchase gives none) and `residency`; `runShops` is the Shops' pass (receipts, holdings, upkeep, dormancy, reactivation; the day-3 warning is flagged). **Deviation, recorded as DK154:** upkeep is its own record rather than a purchase signed by the Shops (credits.md 4.5).
